letting g be mset(2,2)

g = x

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#counts_1: matrix indexed by [int(1..2)] of int(0..2)
  find x#counts_2: matrix indexed by [int(1..2)] of int(1..2)
new constraints:
  and([sum([SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[1]),SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[2]);int(1..)]) >= 0,sum([SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[1]),SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[2]);int(1..)]) <= 4;int(1..)])
  or([SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[1]) > 0,SafeIndex(x#counts_2,[1]) = 1;int(1..)])
  or([SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[2]) > 0,SafeIndex(x#counts_2,[2]) = 1;int(1..)])
  or([SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[2]) = 0,SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[1]) > 0;int(1..)])
  or([SafeIndex(x#counts_1,[2]) = 0,([SafeIndex(x#counts_2,[1]);int(1..)] <lex [SafeIndex(x#counts_2,[2]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
